**No phone number or email address appears anywhere in this list.** This repository holds none, and a plausible-looking clerk's address that nobody verified is a fabricated record — the kind that gets acted on precisely because it looks complete. Every item says what to ask and where the lookup starts.

## What is NOT on this list, and why

Roger is not asked to perform repository, hashing, mapping or promotion work. If an item below would have him touch this repository, it is misfiled.

- **sha calculation** — computing or comparing SHA-256 of a held or fetched binary. Owner: ACQ and PROMO lanes.
- **custody promotion** — moving bytes into governed custody and writing the custody record. Owner: PROMO lanes.
- **corpus indexing** — indexing the Master Library and reconciling the corpus index against held bytes. Owner: Captain.
- **route family mapping** — mapping a route or family to the documents it requires. Owner: DISC lanes.
- **generated record reconciliation** — regenerating the queue, the manifest, the conveyor or the collision record. Owner: Captain.
- **path ownership** — deciding which lane owns which path and resolving collisions. Owner: Captain.
- **legal decisions** — any question of what the law requires. Owner: counsel, through the legal queue.
- **ordinary engineering** — generators, verifiers, workflows, prompts and their tests. Owner: Captain.

104 SOURCE_BLOCKED famil(ies) name no official form at all. Those are DISC discovery obligations -- settling which document a route requires -- and they are lane work, not an errand.


**22 obligation(s) are statutory citations queued as if they were forms** ("LA-CCRP-ART-987"; "LA-CCRP-ART-988"; "LA-CCRP-ART-989"; "LA-CCRP-ART-991"; "LA-CCRP-ART-992"; "LA-CCRP-ART-994"). These are statutory citations, not published forms. There is no PDF at the other end and no clerk who can hand one over; the routes need a custom pleading drafted against the statute. Ranked on a fetch list they would send a person to several offices to ask for something that does not exist. Owner: a packet-build lane, as custom-pleading drafting — not Roger, and not an ACQ lane.

```text
Subject: current official edition of Statement of Inability to Afford Payment of Court Costs or an Appeal Bond (TX)

Good morning —

I am looking for the current official edition of Statement of Inability to Afford Payment of Court Costs or an Appeal Bond, used for record clearing petitions in TX.

Three questions, and I am happy to be pointed at a page rather than sent a file:

  1. Is this still the form the court accepts, or has it been superseded?
  2. Where is the current edition published?
  3. Is the statewide edition the one this court uses, or does this court use its own?

Thank you for your time.

— Roger Roman, LegalEase
```
</details>

- if a clerk gave it to you: who, which office, and what they said about which edition is current

**What does not count:**
- a screenshot, a photograph, or a printout

- a file re-saved or 'optimized' by a PDF editor
- a form found on a county site when the question was the statewide edition, unless you say that is what it is
- an assurance that the form is current without the publisher's own revision line or a clerk's statement
